LEFT JOIN (

FROM "Purchases" AS p
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT e."EventId", e."PurchaseId", e."CreatedAt", e."CreatedBy", e."ModifiedAt", e."ModifiedBy", e."PurchaseDate", e."Rating", e."TicketQuantity", e."UpdatedAt", e0."Id", e0."AvailableTickets", e0."CategoryId", e0."CreatedAt" AS "CreatedAt0", e0."CreatedByUserId", e0."Description", e0."EndDate", e0."EventStatus", e0."StartDate", e0."TicketPrice", e0."Title", e0."UpdatedAt" AS "UpdatedAt0"
